economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.41 / 9 November 2026EDITORIAL
2026 HAS EXPOSED NEW RISKS FOR THE EUROPEAN GAS MARKET
1 C. Grekou, V. Mignon, L. Ragot, 2026, ‘From Russian gas to the global LNG market: The European Union’s new vulnerability’, CEPII, Policy Brief.
2 ACER, 2026, ‘Analysis of the European LNG market development. Monitoring report’.
3 These contracts allow the buyer to choose, or change, the delivery point for the shipment. Recent US contracts (Free On Board and flexible destination) generally

feature these characteristics.
4 IEA, 2026, ‘Global Gas Security Review & Gas Market Report Q4-2026’.
Europe’s vulnerability to the gas market has changed in recent years. Whilst its reliance on imports has remained stable,
its gas supply has been more vulnerable to global market conditions since 2022, due to a shift in the composition of its
imports. Furthermore, in 2026, the combination of high geopolitical risk in one of the main production regions and the

pressure of climate change on European consumers has created an unprecedented situation, leading to price pressures
and significant uncertainty regarding the short-term outlook. Even if they ease, tensions in the European gas market could
persist beyond the winter, largely due to the need to refill inventories.
Whilst Europe is not heavily reliant on gas imports from the Middle
East, it has had to contend with a fresh surge in prices since the out -

break of war in Iran last February, a rise that has, in part, been passed
on to electricity prices this summer. Furthermore, new sources of
vulnerability, particularly those linked to climate change, are at play.
These call for caution regarding the short-term outlook for the Euro -
pean gas market.
EUROPE’S GROWING EXPOSURE TO MARKET VOLATILITY
Unlike the 2022 energy crisis, which hit Europe directly, the war in
Iran is indirectly affecting the European gas market. 80% of gas exports

from the Middle East are destined for Asia. However, the growing inter-
connection between the two main gas-importing regions — Asia and
Europe — has resulted in the crisis spreading to Europe. Since 2022,
the transmission of shocks to liquefied natural gas (LNG) benchmark
prices on the European and Asian markets (TTF and JKM) has increased
significantly, in both directions1.
Despite a fall of around 20% in European gas consumption since 2022,
the replacement of around 60% of Russian pipeline imports with pur -

chases of liquefied natural gas (see Chart 1) has kept Europe’s vulne -
rability at a high level. Given developments in the LNG market —
growing interconnection between European and Asian markets and
the importance of spot contracts (around one-third of European supply
contracts2) — Europe finds itself, through no fault of its own, increa -
singly exposed to gas price volatility.
Furthermore, the increasing autonomy and flexibility of the global
LNG market may exacerbate this volatility. The higher proportion of

LNG sales contracts with flexible destinations3 (rising from 29% to 51%
of the total between 2016 and 2025), linked to the massive influx of
US LNG onto the market, together with the reduction in the share of
contracts indexed to oil prices (from 70% to 55% over the same period),
have made price formation on the LNG market more autonomous, the-
reby increasing the opportunities for arbitrage and the flexibility of this
market.
MAJOR DISRUPTIONS IN THE LNG MARKET HAVE CONSTRAINED THE REFIL-

LING OF EUROPEAN INVENTORIES
The 85% reduction in LNG exports from Qatar and the UAE in Q2 and Q3
2026 was offset by two-thirds through, on the one hand, an increase
in exports from other producing countries (primarily the United States)
and, on the other hand, a fall in demand in Asia and Europe (which
is forecast to reach, respectively, 1.5% and 3% year-on-year in 2026,
according to IEA forecasts4). Owing to the damage caused by the war to
infrastructure (17% of Qatar’s production capacity is out of service) and

logistical constraints specific to the LNG market (unavailability of LNG
carriers), the LNG market has benefited only marginally from the re -
sumption of flows through the Strait of Hormuz since July. Against this
backdrop of tensions in the gas market, high prices in Europe (up by
around 174% since the outbreak of the conflict) have significantly
slowed the refilling of European stocks. They stood at 75% of full capa-
city at the end of October, whilst the official target is 80% (see Chart 2).

OVER THE WINTER, DIVERGENT FACTORS COULD STABILISE PRICES AT A
HIGH LEVEL
Gas stock levels that are below the target set by the European Commis-
sion do not increase the risk of a shortage on the European continent.
Nevertheless, Europe’s vulnerability towards the end of winter, in the
event of a cold snap, would force it to buy on the market at high prices.

Furthermore, from a technical perspective, exposure to the conse -
quences of a cold snap at the end of winter is all the more pronounced
as the rate at which gas can be withdrawn from storage (the ease with
which a country can access its supplies) decreases (due to lower stock
levels in storage facilities).
Apart from stock levels at the start of winter, three factors will deter -

mine the situation at the end of the season, a key period of vulnera -
bility:
1/ the resumption of exports from the Gulf, a region that is essential for
restoring market balance in the short term;
2/ weather conditions during the winter;
3/ renewable energy production.
According to the IEA, in the worst-case scenario (with exports from the
Middle East only resuming from the end of the first quarter of 2027, a
cold winter and low renewable energy production), stocks would stand

at 7% of total capacity by the end of winter, compared with 33% in
the best-case scenario. Such levels remain significantly below the ave-
rage for the last five years at this time of year: 80% in the worst-case
scenario and 18% in the best-case scenario. This would underpin res -
tocking demand over the next two quarters, and therefore keep prices
at a fairly high level. For the time being, a significant recovery in LNG
exports from the Gulf remains highly uncertain, even though indirect

talks between the warring parties have resumed. On a more positive
note, the current development of a very powerful El Niño event should,
in principle, be associated with relatively mild temperatures during the
winter in western Europe. According to the futures markets, European
gas prices are not expected to return to their pre-crisis levels until
2028 (see Chart 3).
ARE WE HEADING FOR A SUMMER PEAK IN GAS DEMAND?
In addition to these factors, which are likely to prolong market tensions

during the first half of 2027, the summer period could also be cha -
racterised by increased demand for gas. During the summer of 2026,
5 In early October 2026, the water levels in French reservoirs reached a ten-year low, standing at 23% below seasonal averages.
6 RTE data.
7 M. Mueller, P . Devaux, October 2026, ‘What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity
continues to turn the screw?’, EcoInsight.

8 Oxford Institute for Energy Studies, October 2026, Quarterly Gas Market Review.
weather conditions hampered the generation of certain forms of re -
newable energy (heatwaves and droughts restricting electricity gene -
ration from hydroelectric5 and wind power sources). They also disrup-
ted nuclear power generation (in France, nuclear electricity generation
fell by around 10% year-on-year between August and October6). Along-
side these constraints on carbon-free electricity generation, heatwaves

are driving up demand for electricity, particularly for air conditioning.
Against this backdrop, as a source of flexibility within the electricity
mix, gas remains a key determinant of electricity prices in Europe 7.
In Q3 2026, whilst the decline in European gas consumption mainly
affected industry, the residential sector and the commercial sector 8,
consumption by gas-fired power stations was 9% higher than in 2025.
STRENGTHENING EUROPEAN PROGRAMMES

In this context, and despite the progress made in decarbonising the
European electricity mix, high gas prices have led to a rise in whole -
sale electricity prices in most European countries. Between July and
September 2026, these prices were on average 54% higher than in the
same period a year earlier.
Against this backdrop of widespread rises in energy prices, European go-
vernments have primarily introduced measures to support households’
purchasing power when buying fuel. Measures relating to gas supply

remain fairly limited. The European Commission has extended the tar-
get for refilling European stocks to 80% (instead of 90%) for 2026. It has
also urged Member States to extend the stock-refill season in order
to smooth out purchase prices. Furthermore, the measures taken in
2022 under the RePower EU programme have been maintained: redu -
cing dependence on Russian gas with the aim of a complete phase-out
(pipeline and LNG) by the end of 2027; and pooling gas purchases at

European level. The ‘decarbonisation and electrification’ component of
this programme was strengthened in April 2026 by the Accelerate EU
programme, notably through measures to accelerate the electrification
of demand and the development of electricity grid infrastructure at
European level.

At national level, it is worth highlighting the price cap mechanism for
gas purchased by power stations, which the Italian government is set
to introduce. In 2025, gas set the price of electricity in Italy in 73% of
cases (compared with an average of 40% across Europe). This price cap
mechanism, similar to that introduced in Spain in 2022, is, in principle,

only sustainable in the short term. Indeed, its cost is likely to be signi-
ficant given the Italian electricity mix’s high dependence on gas.
*****
Increased market dependence and the consequences of rising climate
risk for European electricity generation are altering the vulnerabilities
associated with the continent’s gas market. The sources of risk are
increasing in number and becoming harder to manage. This calls for
the continuation and strengthening of decarbonisation efforts in the

European policies launched in 2022.
Pascal Devaux
pascal.devaux@bnpparibas.com

台積電 (2330  TT)  Trading Buy

AI 投資 放緩 疑慮 ， 建議 Trading Buy 。  
投 資 建 議 ： 儘管消費性 (如 PC 、 智 慧 型 手 機)與 價 格 敏 感 終 端 市
場 面 臨 零 組 件 價 格 上 漲 與 總 體 經 濟 不 確 定 性 之 挑 戰 ，台 積 電 對
2027 年 對此 抱 持 審 慎 樂觀 態 度 ， 但 受 惠 於 領 先 先 進 製 程 之 技 術
差 異 化 與 廣 泛 客 戶 基 礎 ， 及 代 理 人 AI(Agentic AI) 推動 AI 助理
普 及 、 開 源 模 型 降 低 使 用 門 檻 ， 進 一 步 刺 激CPU 與 網 路 晶 片 需
求，預 估 台 積 電 2027 年稅後 EPS 147.10 元。 長 期 而 言，台 積 電
憑 藉 在 先 進 製 程 與 先 進 封 裝的 高 市 佔 率，實 質 基 本 面 依然 強 勁。
然 而 ， 美 國 民 主 黨 主 導 國 會 後 ，有 可 能 因 環 境 能 耗 與 安 全 疑 慮
對 AI 數 據 中 心 及 尖 端 模 型 祭 出 強 硬 監 管，並 取 消 稅 收 補 貼。引
發 市 場 擔 憂 科 技 巨 頭 放 緩 AI 資 本 支 出，間 接 影 響 台 積 電 的 業 績
成 長 動 能 ， 因 此 建 議 台 積 電 Trading Buy ， 目 標 價 2500 元。  
3Q2 6 稅後 EPS 29.43 元 ： 3Q26 營收 1.49 兆 元 ，QoQ+17.62% ；
以 美 元 計 價 營 收 為 467.2 億 美 元 ，QoQ+16.20% ， 皆 優 於 財 測 上
緣 。 毛 利 率 為 66.41% ，較 2Q26 減少 1.30 個 百 分 點 ， 主 因 受 到
N2 量 產 初 期 之 利 潤 稀 釋 影 響。台積電 3Q2 6 稅後 EPS 29.43 元。 
2026 年 資本支出 60 0~ 64 0 億美元 ： AI 需 求 持 續 ， 台 積 電 提 高
2026 年本支出 600 ~640 億美元， YoY+46.72% ~+56.50% ， 較 原
先 預 估 增 加 約 15% 。其 中 先 進 製 程 N3 和 N2 製 程 產 能 的 增 加 將
占 總 資 本 支 出 比 重 70~80% 。 規 劃 全 球 新 增 3 座 晶 圓 廠 以 支 援
N3 技 術 之 強 大 需 求 ： 台 南 科 學 園 區 追 加 興 建 另 一 座N3 新 廠 ，
N2 製 程 在 2027 年 產 能 倍 增 ， 美 國 廠 提 前 至 3Q27 營運 。  
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 32.64 元：高 階 製 程 持 續 帶 動 台 積 電 營 收 成
長， 台 積 電 預 估 3Q26 毛 利 率 將 介 於 65% -67% ，以中間值 66% 
計算，較 3Q26 下降約 0.4 個 百 分 點 。 主 要 是 因 為 N2 技 術 的 快
速 爬 坡 ， 預 期 將 稀 釋 毛 利 率 約 3 個 百 分 點 ， 此 稀 釋 將 被 領 先 技
術 的 強 勁 需 求 與 持 續 的 成 本 改 善 ( 含 生 產 力 提 升 ) 部 分 抵 消 。 預
估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 32 .64 元。 2026 年 EPS 111.39 元。

  台積 電 ESG ：  
ESG 政 策 重 點 
•  2025 年起，所有關鍵排放源供應商必須簽署溫室氣體減排協議，承諾
2030 年 達 成 減 排 目 標 ， 且 2026 年 底 前 要 完 成 產 品 碳 足 跡 第 三 方 驗 證 ，
不 合 規 者 將 減 少 合 作 機 會 。 
•  將 供 應 鏈 減 碳績 效 全 面 納 入 選 商 評 估，每 年 須 提 出 新 年 度 與 中 長 期 減 排
計 畫 ， 定 期 檢 討 減 碳 進 度 ， 強 化 全 產 業 鏈 去 碳 化 管 理 。 
•  推 出 供 應 鏈 減 碳 行 動 指 引，包 括 績 效 追 蹤 與 提 高 透 明 度、資 源 支 援 與 輔
導 、 提 升 動 機 與 擴 大 影 響 範 圍 、 合 作 創 新 與 成 本 效 益 管 理 四 大 面 向 。 
近期 ESG 行 動 與 報 告 
•  台 積 電 發 表《 永 續 報 告 書 》， 強 化ESG 治 理 架 構 及 與 利 害 關 係 人 合 作 ，
揭 露 永 續 進 展 。 
•  擴 大 永 續 報 告 內 容 ， 聚 焦 九 項 聯 合 國SDGs 與 多 元 共 融 、 多 語 、 無 障 礙
專 案 ， 包 括 女 性 領 導 力 、 多 元 文 化 主 題 活 動 和 身 心 障 礙 者 職 場 體 驗 ， 提
高 員 工 共 融 意 識 。 
•  ESG 官網於 2024 年 底 改 版，減 少 數 位 能 耗 9%、碳 排 放 28% ，網 站 對 身
心 障 礙 者 資 訊 近 用 性 提 升 ， 導 入 多 語 系 發 展 及 多 元 功 能 服 務 。 
長 期 承 諾 與 具 體 成 果 
•  加速 RE100 進 程 ， 將 全 球 營 運100% 再 生 能 源 目 標 從 2050 年 提 前 至 2040
年， 2030 年 生 產 據 點 再 生 能 源 比 例 提 升 至 60% ， 並 推 出 減 碳 補 助 專 案
總額 8,400 萬 元 ， 帶 動 供 應 商 節 電 、 節 水 、 減 廢。  
•  治 理 政 策 由 ESG 指 導 委 員 會 及 ESG 委 員 會 主 導 ， 確 立 重 大 議 題 盤 點 與
風 險 機 會 管 理，每 年 發 布 永 續 報 告 書 作 為 管 理 工 具，連 結 高 階 主 管 薪 酬
與 ESG 成 果 ， 並 透 過 ESG AWARD 獎 勵 機 制 推 動 內 部 文 化 
•  秉 持 創 辦 人 張 忠 謀 價 值 觀(誠 信 正 直 、 強 化 環 保 、 關 懷 弱 勢)， 跨 部 門 督
導 23 項 永 續 作 為 及 57 個 長 期 目 標 ， 對 應 九 個 聯 合 國 SDGs ， 確 保 透 明
揭 露 與 利 害 關 係 人 溝 通 。 
•  強 化 全 球 價 值 鏈 治 理 ， 呼 應 國 際 ESG 倡 議 ， 持 續 精 進 評 比 成 績 ， 如 連
續 入 選 道 瓊 永 續 指 數。

6 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15 %

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following its results release on 30 October, we raise our 
forecasts and 12M TP for Global Unichip (GUC). The stock more than 
doubled since our last update (Upgrading: unleashed capacity adding to 
growth, 5 August), yet its recent pullback amid increased market volatility 
looks to be unlocking value again for investment, so we reaffirm our Buy (1) 
rating. Fundamentals have unfolded strongly. According to our market 
research, GUC’s key customer in the AI CPU space has pulled forward its 
order trajectory ahead of the previous schedule amid accelerated demand 
for internal and external AI datacentre build-out. This comes on top of its 
initial ramp of another new accelerator project, followed by the next 
generation AI CPU and accelerator ramps for the same customer, with 
further upside from another new AI accelerator customer in the auto space. 
We suggest investors take advantage of the pullback to bargain-hunt.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes. In light of its key AI CPU customer 
accelerating its ordering trajectory, together with multiple AI projects 
ramping in the next 1-2 years to fire on all cylinders for growth, we lift our 
2026-28E EPS by 8-50% for GUC. We forecast triple-digit % YoY EPS 
growth over 2Q26-2Q27, before moderating to 50% or so into 2028. While 
such strong growth should help sustain its hefty valuation, further rerating 
may be unlikely as this would depend on GUC’s ability to win more AI 
projects than we expect, and more importantly, whether it is able to secure 
enough capacity from the supply chain to accommodate any incremental 
business amid the constrained supply environment.

Margin-accretive at operating level. On top of the AI CPU demand uplift, 
we expect GUC to have a total of 6 major AI projects in mass-production for 
2027, including 4 CPU and 2 accelerators, all for US-based hyperscalers or 
system vendors mainly focusing on N3 process nodes, helping broaden 
GUC’s AI project base. Although these projects appear margin-dilutive at 
gross level, they should be accretive at the operating level, thanks to 
GUC’s prudent opex management on enhanced operating leverage.

What we recommend: Given our forecast changes, we lift our 12M TP for 
GUC to TWD7,800 (previous: TWD5,125), based on a higher 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 67x (previous: 63x), justified by its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 66% from a PEG perspective. Although further rerating remains 
challenging, we are comfortable with our Buy (1) call due to the recent 
share price pullback unlocking value for investment again. Key downside 
risks to our call would be supply constraints and margin execution.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 23-37% below the consensus 
forecasts as we factor in the supply constraints that will moderate growth.

4 November 2026

Value returns when jitters rise

➢ Lifting forecasts on AI strength and a broadened project base 
➢ Margin dilutive at gross level yet accretive at operating level 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) with a higher 12M TP of TWD7,800

Company profile 
Founded in 1998, GUC is an IC design service provider focusing on AI/HPC, networking, consumer and 
industrial applications.

### Page 5

5

Global Unichip (3443 TT): 4 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
GUC's Board of Directors (BoD) comprises 8 members, including 4 independent directors (50%
of the board), which is significantly higher than the regulatory requirement of at least 20%. The
Board demonstrates diverse expertise across legal, finance, technology, and business
management. The Chairman and the President are different individuals, ensuring separation of
roles and enhancing governance independence.
Capital management 1
Over the past decade, GUC has maintained a consistently high dividend payout ratio, generally
above 60%, and in most years exceeding 80%. In 2025, the company distributed TWD2,144m in
cash dividends, reflecting its policy of sustaining or increasing dividend amounts over time.
Given GUC’s asset-light business model as an IC design service provider, which does not
require significant capital expenditure for manufacturing facilities, this high payout ratio is
appropriate and aligns with its long-term commitment to shareholder returns.
Related party &
transaction 1
GUC's sole foundry supplier is TSMC, who is also GUC's largest shareholder. As an IC design
service company without onsite production, GUC's relationship with the leading foundry of
advanced process nodes provides it a key competitive advantage, in our view.
S Supply chain management 2
GUC is committed to maintaining long-term partnerships with suppliers and establishing a
sustainable supply chain through measures such as promoting its green action policy and
concept to the entire supply chain, where all its suppliers are required to follow its Responsible
Business Alliance (RBA) regulation. It also periodically conducts supplier audits to ensure the
quality of suppliers.
E Water & wastewater management 1
GUC continues its green living project to reduce water consumption. In 2024, the Company
maintained water-saving measures such as rainwater collection systems, automatic sensor
faucets, dual-flush toilets, and regular inspections of water equipment. These efforts aim to
improve water efficiency and promote conservation across operations.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
As an IC design service provider, GUC does not have in-house manufacturing facilities. It
requires its suppliers to use recycled materials for on-site operation and transportation, and
reduce the amount of expendable resources being used. In order to promote a paperless office,
GUC has established a cloud operating system to reduce the use of paper-based documents.
E GHG emissions 1
GUC adopts energy-saving measures to reduce greenhouse gas emissions and has set annual
electricity conservation targets. In 2024, the Company achieved a 1% reduction in electricity use
and increased green electricity to 39% of total consumption. It also continues to develop low-
power and high-efficiency IP to help minimise environmental impact.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 3

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
A Closer Look at the Fundamentals
Why we changed our minds – March caution, May upside
surprise
We downgraded Winbond and Nanya Tech in March (Old Memory: Turning Selective) as
elevated DDR4 pricing encouraged supply responses and customer pushback, pointing to
narrower shortages and moderating price hikes. In May, we reversed those calls as lower
expected supply from Micron and Hynix, alongside stronger server and enterprise SSD

demand, widened the projected supply-demand gap (Old Memory: Upside Surprise
Ahead). The distinction matters: March was a selective DDR4 call; today, we see a broader
challenge to old memory’s pricing durability.
What has changed
Pricing power cannot offset weaker demand indefinitely
Our constructive thesis was that niche memory, with limited fixed-price L TA constraints,
could reprice faster and outperform mainstream memory. We now see that flexibility

becoming a two-way risk. Rising uncertainty over cloud spending weakens confidence in
incremental enterprise demand, while much weaker consumer demand challenges
customers’ ability to absorb further hikes.
After the US midterms, our US team now expects ~20% of 2028e US
data center capacity additions to shift to 2029
This include some projects in Texas and Ohio, as governors introduce new data center
legislation (refer to Buy the Drop: AI Investment Opportunities Post the US Midterms by

Stephen Byrd). Stephen has shifted ~5GW of US data center capacity additions from 2028
to 2029  (Exhibit 11 ) – equivalent to 20% of US and 10% of global 2028 capacity
additions. This implies a 4% Y/Y decline in US data center GW additions  in 2028 before a
38% Y/Y rise in 2029. We now expect global data center additions to increase 9% in 2028
and    20% Y/Y  in 2029.
This could create an air pocket across the tech supply chain in 2028,
potentially reducing pricing power and leading to an inventory build

Our latest PC semis work highlights procurement pull-ins in 1Q–3Q26, followed by a
deeper correction in 4Q, reinforcing the risk that earlier orders overstated underlying
consumption (PC Semis: More Divergent Outlooks).
Supply-demand update –  looking beyond the shortage
Our revised models for SLC NAND, MLC NAND, NOR, and DDR4 point to progressively
more balanced markets, particularly into 2028. We now expect 2028 supply-demand
deficits of -3%, -5%, and -1% for SLC NAND, MLC NAND, NOR, with a +1% excess for DDR4.

Near-term tightness need not disappear immediately for stocks to de-rate: a narrowing
shortage can reduce incremental pricing power before earnings peak. We therefore place

4
less weight on extrapolating recent ASP gains and more on normalized demand and
supply responses and sustainable profitability.

Winbond: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
We adjust our EPS estimates +8% for 2026, -9% for 2027, and -22% for 2028:  The
2026 EPS increase reflects 3Q26 actual results. We also factor in a potential peak in
pricing power across NOR, SLC, and DDR4. Compared to the previous estimate, we now
reduce our flash wafer ASP assumptions throughout 2027-2028 and  26% into 4Q28, and
reduce our DRAM ASP per bit estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  23% into 4Q28,

without changing our flash or DRAM shipment estimates. Apart from reduced revenue
estimates, we also lower our gross margin estimates to account for the reduced ASP
estimates. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of changes in
our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make these
changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potentially delays in 2028 US cloud capex.

Winbond: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to  NT$120
We continue to apply P/B multiple methodology to derive our price target, in line with our
approach for Greater China memory IDM peers and in view of the industry's high earnings
volatility. Our price target (base case scenario value) is set at 1.2x 2027e BVPS vs. our
prior target multiple of 2.9x 2027e BVPS, to reflect the potential peak in pricing power.
Our 1.2x target P/B is above our 1.0x P/B valuation anchor –  we still favor the company's

CUBE and S-SiCap businesses for their long-term potential. We see 1.0x P/B as the
downside valuation anchor because book value provides a psychological support level for
capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during cyclical downturns. When earnings are
temporarily depressed, investors typically place greater weight on the underlying net
asset base.
Our base case value/price target cut of 58% is almost entirely a function  of reduced target
multiple, rather than change in 2027e BVPS, which declines only 2% in our new estimate.

Our bull case value is reduced from NT$342 to NT$180, and our bear case value is
reduced from NT$118 to NT$50, based on 1.8x and 0.5x 2027e BVPS, respectively
(previously 3.4x and 1.2x).
We downgrade Winbond to EW
We continue to like the CUBE and S-SiCap businesses for longer-term potential, but we
see near-term pressure from NOR, SLC NAND, and DDR4 businesses, in all of which
industry supply is ramping faster than demand is increasing, especially as global cloud

capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029. We believe the stock still has strong
valuation support at 1.0-1.2x P/B, and therefore we believe an EW rating is fair.

GigaDevice: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
We cut our EPS estimates  25% for 2026, 36% for 2027, and 33% for 2028:  The 2026e
EPS change mainly reflects actual 2Q26 and 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e EPS
changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across DDR4, NOR Flash, and SLC NAND.
We now reduce our DRAM and others revenue estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 43%
into 4Q28, mainly reflecting changes in our DDR4 ASP estimates. We also reduce our

Flash (including NOR and SLC NAND) ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  25% into
4Q28, without changing our Flash shipment estimates. We did not materially change our
GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of
changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make
these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

GigaDevice: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target  to Rmb259
We continue to derive our price target, which is our base case value, from a residual
income model. We believe this best captures the stock’s long-term value.
Our base case value cut of 65% can be attributed ~33% to earnings estimate revisions, as
has been elaborated, and ~33% from multiple compression, driven by intermediate growth
rate cut and beta increase.  (Note: the overall price target change is calculated from the

fully revised RI model. The individual contribution estimates are illustrative and should
not be added together or compounded to derive the overall change.).
We cut our intermediate growth rate to 8.0% from 11.3% to reflect stronger changes in
the supply environment compared to the end market demand over the medium to long
term. The potential peak in pricing power for niche memory (DDR4, SLC, MLC, NOR) and
the potential delay of 2028 US cloud capex now suggest slower revenue growth for

GigaDevice from 2026 to 2036. We think a 3.3ppt p.a. growth rate cut reflects the new
supply/demand dynamics with a closer gap.
We also raise our beta to 1.50 from 1.14, leading our cost of equity assumption to rise to
11.8% from 9.4%. This  reflects a cautious valuation scenario, to account for the recent
memory segment volatility, 70%+ correction from the stock price peak in June, and the
fabless model, which does not provide a floor support for valuation similar to the IDM

model. The elevated beta reflects the company's exposure to rapidly changing niche
memory pricing dynamics that could severely affect earnings with a downward bias
compared to our financial forecasts.
Our other key assumptions remain unchanged, including equity risk premium of 6.5%, risk-
free rate of 2% and payout ratio of 40%.
Our bull case value falls to Rmb448 from Rmb1,300, and our bear case value to Rmb112
from Rmb324.
We downgrade GigaDevice to EW

We still see the company as a domestic leader in MCU localization and believe its
customized DRAM business could show a revenue ramp over the next 1-2 years. We also
still like its strategic partnership with CXMT, which provides both capacity support and
process tech improvements. However, we see near-term pressure from the NOR, SLC
NAND, and DDR4 businesses, in all of which industry supply is ramping faster than
demand increase, especially as global cloud capex may face partial delay from 2028 to

2029. Fabless design houses do not face depreciation and utilization issues during a down-
cycle, but at the same time they lack a similar valuation anchor as IDM/foundry players
from their book value, and therefore we see potentially continued high volatility ahead.
Our new price target implies a 2027e P/E of 9x, below the historical average of 38x since
2016, owing to changes in pricing power.

PSMC: Valuation     Methodology
Our price target  is cut to NT$54
We incorporate our revised estimates and maintain our P/B valuation methodology. Our
new target, which is our base case value,  is based on a 2027e P/B of 1.0x, down from 2.0x
previously –  below the historical average of 1.5x since 2021, to reflect pricing
uncertainties.
Our base case value/price target cut of 51% is almost entirely a result of target multiple
reduction, rather than change in 2027e BVPS, which declined only 1.5% in our new

estimate. For PSMC, we use 1.0x P/B as the downside valuation anchor –  book value
provides a psychological support level for capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during
cyclical downturns. When earnings are temporarily depressed, investors typically place
greater weight on the underlying net asset base.
We decrease our bull case value from NT$177 to NT$86 and our bear case value from NT
$55 to NT$27, implying 1.6x and 0.5x 2027e P/B, respectively.

We downgrade PSMC to EW
We are positive on the company's emerging 3D AI foundry business, and we believe PSMC
is well positioned to benefit from its Micron partnership, including front-end migration to
the one-year  process node and back-end opportunities in advanced DRAM production
(e.g., HBM). The company is also catching up with the DRAM pricing up-cycle.
At the same time, its logic foundry business is set to enjoy continued price hikes into 2027.

However, on its memory foundry side, we see near-term pressure from DDR3/4, in which
industry supply is ramping faster than demand is increasing, especially as global cloud
capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029.
We believe the stock still has strong valuation support at 1.0x P/B, and therefore we
believe an EW rating is fair.

Macronix: Earnings  Estimate Revisions
We cut our 2026, 2027, and 2028 EPS forecasts 14%, 20%, and 21%, respectively: The
2026e EPS change mainly reflects actual 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e EPS
changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across NOR and MLC/SLC NAND. We
now reduce our NAND and /NOR wafer ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 15%
and 8% into 4Q28 respectively, without changing our wafer shipment estimates. We did

not materially change our GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are
largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a
good time to make these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating
price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Macronix: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$90 from NT$220
We continue to use a P/B multiple methodology to derive our price target, which is our
base case value, in line with our approach for Greater China memory IDM peers and in
view of the industry's high volatility. Our new target is based on a 2027e P/B of 1.0x, down
from 2.1x previously – below the average of 1.5x since 2017, given the potential peak in
pricing power for NOR and SLC/MLC NAND. For Macronix, we now use 1.0x P/B as the

downside valuation anchor because book value provides a psychological support level for
capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during cyclical downturns. When earnings are
temporarily depressed, investors typically place greater weight on the underlying net
asset base.
Our base case value/price target cut of 59% reflects both target multiple reduction and a
15% 2027 BVPS estimate reduction (relatively large because of a smaller base).
We cut our bull case value from NT$355 to NT$145, which is 1.6x our 2027e BVPS. We cut

our bear case value from NT$100 to NT$41, which is 0.5x our 2027e BVPS.
We downgrade Macronix to EW
We continue to like its unique position in MLC NAND as the only major supplier still
ramping up capacity. However, we do see near-term pressure from NOR, DDR3/4, and
even SLC/MLC NAND, in all of which  industry supply is ramping faster than demand is
increasing, especially as global cloud capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029. We
believe the stock still has strong valuation support at 1.0x P/B, and therefore we believe

an EW rating is fair.

Nanya Tech: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
Our 2026 estimates are unchanged;  we cut our 2027 and 2028 EPS estimates  7% and
9%,respectively: We factor in a potential peak in DDR3/4 pricing power, which affects
Nanya Tech's top line and margins. We now reduce our Nanya Tech DRAM ASP per bit
estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  6% into 4Q28, without changing our wafer
shipment estimates. We did not materially change our GM assumptions. Our changes in

net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross
profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make these changes now as a result of the
narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Morgan Stanley Research 35
Nanya Tech: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from NT$600 to NT$550
Our price target (base case scenario value) reflects a target BVPS multiple of 2.04x for
2027 (was 4.47x) versus ROE of 59% for 2027.  We lower our target multiple to reflect
potential peak in DDR4 pricing power and recent memory segment volatility. For Nanya
Tech, our new base case target P/B multiple of 2.04x P/B is stronger than our downside

valuation anchor of 1.0x P/B for capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks.  This is because we
expect Nanya to remain relatively resilient compared to other DDR4 players in our
coverage, given stronger process technology and potential DDR5 migration.
Our base case value/price target cut of 8% reflects both target multiple reduction and
BVPS estimate adjustment of 3% arising from earnings estimate revisions.
We cut our bull and bear case values from NT$1,270 and NT$330 to NT$1,150 and NT

$300, respectively -- 4.27x and 1.12x 2027e BVPS.
We remain OW
Despite potentially peaking pricing power seen in DDR4, we remain positive on DDR5 in
view of continued strong demand from servers. Nanya is already producing DDR5 on its
second-generation 10-nm process, with DDR5 contributing ~10% of revenue; its next
process enters customer qualification in 1Q27, and EUV implementation is targeted for
2028. This establishes its edge in process technology vs the other DDR4 vendors in our

coverage and a stronger position in DDR5 migration. Valuation is also attractive, in our
view. Therefore, we remain OW on Nanya Tech.
Exhibit 35: Nanya Tech –  Historical P/B band

AP Memory: Estimate revisions summary
We  adjust our EPS estimates  +21% for 2026, +2% for 2027, and -12% for 2028: We
factor in 3Q26 preliminary results. We adjust our 2028 forecasts  mainly to account for
supply chain risks. We note that the company's IoTRAM is less affected by  volatility of
typical commodity DRAM pricing. The 2027e and 2028e EPS changes mainly reflect
supply chain uncertainties around S-SiCap. We now reduce our S-SiCap unit shipment

estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  50%+ into 4Q28. We also slightly reduced  our IoT
RAM ASP estimates throughout 2027-28 to reflect peaking DDR3/4 pricing power. In
addition, we reduced our GM assumptions across product lines to reflect potential  peak in
pricing power and supply chain uncertainties. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates
are largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is
a good time to make these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating

price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Phison: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from NT$2,588 to NT$1,357
Our price target is our base case value, derived from a residual income (RI) model. Our cut
reflects our latest earnings estimates. We also cut our terminal growth rate assumption
from 4.0% to 3.0% to be broadly in line with a conservative estimate of long-term
nominal economic growth in developed economies. We believe this is appropriate for

Phison because rising storage demand should support continued growth, while declining
NAND prices per bit, competition, and maturing SSD penetration should limit the
conversion of volume growth into earnings growth, bringing its growth closer to that of
the broader economy over time.
Our other RI parameters are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 9.0%, a medium-
term growth rate of 9.0%, and a dividend payout ratio assumption of 50.0%.
Apart from the earnings estimate revisions and terminal growth rate change, a material

part of the price target change is driven by an error in the previously published residual
income model (August 2026). The error has now been corrected. In the previous RI model,
the 2028e net income was mistakenly calculated as 2027e net income * (1+ intermediate
growth rate), where it should have been linked to the actual 2028e net income forecast.
This overstated the previous price target.
Our earnings estimates have also changed materially  from the previously published

version: they increase 12% for 2026, 60% for 2027, and 37% for  2028. This mainly reflects
higher GM assumptions of 61.2%, 51.9%, and 42.0%, respectively; previously we assumed
60.1%, 43.4%, and 39.5%.
Our base case value/price target is lowered 48%, of which roughly +37% is contributed by
earnings estimate revisions, -6% is contributed by terminal growth rate reduction, and -
83% is contributed by the correction of the prior RI model error. (The overall price target

change is calculated from the fully revised RI model. The individual contribution estimates
are illustrative and should not be added together or compounded to derive the overall
change.)
We cut our bull and bear case values from NT$3,400 and NT$1,250 to NT$1,782 and NT
$655, respectively, implying 8.4x 2027e EPS in the bull case and 3.1x 2027e EPS in the
bear case.
We downgrade Phison to UW
The company hopes its AiDaptive module and service business can pick up and offset the

margin pressure in the conventional module business in the coming quarters, which we
believe is worth monitoring. However, its 3Q GM  of 59% missed the Street estimate of
60.3%; we see this as a potential sign that the previously strong margin profile supported
by low-cost inventory may start to face more downside. Specifically, the AI storage part of
its business (eSSD, and cSSD for AI PC, together 40% of revenue) may face margin
decline. This is what we have been cautious about, and we see the 3Q margin miss as

having provided a good opportunity to downgrade this stock, which we view as

overvalued based on our revised assumptions.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
78 
삼성전자  (005930) 
2026.11.04

STRESS TEST 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

목표주가 37만원, ‘28년 기준으로는 56만원까지 열림 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 37만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27 년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3 배를 적용했다. 이는 ‘00년 이후 25년간 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 
해당한다[표1]. ‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 56%, 5 9%, 4 8%로 지난 25년 평균인 
22%를 크게 상회할 전망이며, 고원형 ROE가 장기간 유지될 것으로 판단한다.  
이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해질 경우 시장의 시야는 ‘28년으로 향할 것이다. 
이 경우 ‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 56만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 43% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -41%를 넘어섰다. 특히 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
이러한 우려가 지속되는 한, 이익의 눈높이가 높아질수록 멀티플은 저렴해질 
뿐이며, DRAM 가격이 예상보다 더 올라갈 것이라는 소식이 주가의 촉매로 
작용하지 못한다. 결국 주가 반등을 위해서는 시장이 상정하고 있는 다운사이클이 
우려만큼 깊지 않을 것이라는 믿음이 필요한 것으로 본다.

STRESS TEST와 HBM 재평가 
① 당사는 고객들이 어느 정도 가격대에서 LTA를 해지하려는 유인이 생길지 
추정해봤다. Micron의 코멘트를 차용하여 ‘22~’23년 수준의 스트레스 테스트를 
시나리오 분석한 결과, 4Q27 고점 대비 DRAM 가격이 60% 수준 빠져야 해지가 
검토될 수 있다[그림2]. 그마저도 다음 공급 부족기의 물량 배정 우선순위 상실 
등 정성적 요인까지 감안하면 실제 해지 가능성은 더욱 낮은 것으로 판단한다. 
② HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 
중장기적으로 확대될 cHBM은 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 
재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 물량과 가격이 결정된다. 수요가 둔화되더라도 범용 
메모리처럼 재고 누적과 가격 급락으로 이어지기보다는 발주 조정이나 계약 
재협상을 통해 영향을 받는 구조다.  
동사 HBM 매출은 ‘26년 28조원에서 ‘28년 215조원으로 증가할 전망이다. 
‘27년 HBM 가격 방향성의 윤곽이 잡힌 현 시점에서, 동사 HBM 매출의 변수는 
가격이 아니라 물량이며, 물량은 고객과 사전에 협의되는 구조다. 과거 메모리 
업황에서는 2년 뒤의 이익을 가늠하기 어려웠으나, 이제는 HBM을 통해 중장기 
실적을 논의할 수 있게 됐다는 점, 그 자체에 주목한다. 
만약 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E (21배, 15배, 12배)를 
적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 19%, 69%, 108%를 설명한다[표3]. 
특히 cHBM이 본격화할 ‘28년에는 HBM만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명될 수 
있다는 의미다. 시장이 통상 받아들이는 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 다르지 
않다. 
(계속)

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간일 전망이다.  
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 업황 지속성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 이 불확실성이 
멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 
구간이 이어질 전망이다. 기회비용은 결국 환원수익률로 보상받아야 하므로, ‘27년 1월 신규 
주주환원 정책이 중요한 변곡점이 될 것으로 판단한다.  
당사는 동사가 ‘27~’29년 FCF의 50%를 주주에게 환원하고, 이 중 70%를 배당, 30%를 
자사주 매입/소각으로 쓰는 것으로 가정하여, 현재 주가 기준 ‘27년 15% 수준의 
주주환원수익률을 기대한다. 미국 10년물 국채 금리가 5% 안팎인 환경에서 무위험 금리의 
3배에 달하는 환원수익률, 그중 배당만으로 10% 수준의 수익률은 그 자체로 희소한 투자 
포인트로 볼 수 있다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는, 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 이후 신규 팹 물량이 
소량 출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 LTA가 이익을 지킨다는 사실이 
숫자로 확인되고 cHBM의 가치가 재평가된다면 순환 할인이 축소될 것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

### Page 1

삼성바이오로직스 (207940/KS) 
3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

COMPLIANCE NOTICE 
작성자(이선경)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

Document 267: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면  이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%) 
ㆍSTRONG BUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +50%이상 3% 
ㆍBUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +15%이상 ~ +50%미만 94% 
ㆍHOLD(중립) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%이상 ∼ +15%미만 3% 
ㆍREDUCE(매도) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%미만 0% 
(2026.09.30 기준)

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지

목표주가 540,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.28) 
285,500 
조선업종

◼ 글로벌 피어 평균 멀티플 하락을 반영하여 목표주가 하향 
◼ 3Q에 산재한 일회성 요인. 원화강세, 조업일수 감소, 임단협 비용 반영 
◼ 모두의 우려에도 불구하고 본업은 안정적으로 천천히 잘 진행되는 중

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 540,000원으로 -32.5% 하향 
목표주가는 28F EPS 42,343원에 글로벌 조선사 피어 평균 12.8배를 적용해 
산출함. 목표주가 하향은 글로벌 경기 불확실성과 중국 조선소 생산캐파 확장
으로 인한 공급과잉 리스크로 전반적인 산업 멀티플이 하락한 것을 반영함. 
다만 가스선 수주 모멘텀과 발전용 엔진의 수주 및 생산 확대 등에 따른 중장
기 성장 유효

3분기는 원래 비수기입니다 
동사의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영
업이익은 8,992억원(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ), OPM 15.6%를 기록. 3분기는 
1)원달러 환율 하락(전분기 대비 평균 -79원) 2)연휴로 인한 조업일수 감소(47
일), 3)임단협 관련 추가 발생 가능 비용 75% 반영)의 영향이 가장 컸음

중장기적인 모멘텀은 여전히 살아있다 
[상선] 중국 공급 확대에 따른 선가 및 수주 경쟁 심화는 부담이나 LNG선 발
주 재개 및 노후선 교체 수요는 긍정적. 동사의 고부가가치선 위주의 수주 전
략 방향은 유지될 예정 [해양] 루야 프로젝트 진행률 인식으로 4분기도 양호
한 이익률 기대, 다만 현재 수주 부진으로 블록, 혹은 LNG선 투입을 통해 매
출 하락을 방어하는 방안도 구상 중 [방산] 키윗과의 미국 합작 조선소를 통해 
콘솔탱커, 로로선 등 지원함 건조를 시작으로 전투함까지 확장 준비 [엔진] 국
가전력망 및 데이터센터향 발전 수요 확대, 하이퍼스케일러 직접협의로 성장
성 강화, 수주 확정 및 추진 물량 기준 28년 1.5GW, 29년 3GW 생산 계획 중

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Company Brief 
이차전지/석유화학 
Analyst 노우호 
wooho.rho@meritz.co.kr 
RA 황현식 
hyeonsig.hwang@meritz.co.kr Long Trading 구간  
3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후: ESS ↑, BEV ↓  
LG에너지솔루션의 3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후 배터리 섹터는 11/3 미국 중간선거
를 전후로 Trading Long 투자의견이 유효하다. 미국 중간선거 이후 특정정당 우세와 
별개로, 미국 Ex-China ESS 공급망 프리미엄은 유효하다. 동사 역시 그간의 ESS 
사업 난제(단위당 수율, 팩 병목)는 점진적 해소될 점이다. 반면 시장의 BEV 반등 기
대감에 동의하지 않는다. EU 프랑스와 독일의 내연기관 퇴출시점 지연 움직임 및 미
국 연비규제 정책 폐지 결정으로 전통 완성차 고객사들향 BEV 배터리 출하 부진은 
여전하겠다. 미국의 BEV 정책 변화시점은 28.11월 대선으로 지연될 전망이다.

ESS 모멘텀: 한국 제3차 ESS 중앙계약시장 사업자 선정 모멘텀 
대다수 시장 참여자들은 2026년 연말까지 LG에너지솔루션의 미국 ESS 60Gwh  규
모의 잔여 수주 달성 가시성에 집중한다. 해당 ESS 사업 수주 가이던스 관건은 시기
보다 방향성이다. 단, 당사가 주목하는 연말 모멘텀은 한국 정부향 제3차 ESS 중앙
계약시장 사업자 선정으로 판단한다. 3차 규모는 1.13Gw로 1&2차 계약 규모(합산 
1.13Gw) 대비 확대되었다. 또한 언론보도상 LG에너지솔루션은 해당 수주를 위한 핵
심 4대 소재 등 전 Value-chain 국산화를 선언한 바 있다. 해당 수주 확정시, 동사
를 비롯한 해당 Value-chain에 긍정적 모멘텀이 예상된다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 51만원 제시   
26년 연중 지정학 Risk, 에너지 대전환 영향 등에 전력 인프라 수요는 꾸준할 전망
이다. 향후 국내 이차전지 산업은 ‘L’ Shaped Cycle 전망, 반면 동사는 스마트카 T사
향 원통형 전지 출하량 증가, ESS 제조업 이상으로 변모하는 과정으로 산업 평균 성
장률 Outperform 가능하다. 투자의견 Buy 유지, 적정주가는 추정치 상향에 51만원
을 제시한다.

Meritz Research  2026. 10. 15

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 510,000원 
현재주가 (10.14) 422,000원 
상승여력 20.9%

Document 296: Anthropic路演PPT：“算力”提了49次、“安全”27次，收入一年翻12倍，长期利润率目标35%

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic路演PPT：“算力”提了49次、“安全”27次，收入一年翻12倍，长期利润率目标35%.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

Anthropic IPO 路演视频中 “ 算⼒ ” 被提及 49 次， " 安全 " 相关词 27 次； CEO 开场约 30 秒就提
到⽣物武器⻛险。 PPT 显⽰，第三季度收⼊ 169.56 亿美元，同⽐增超 12 倍，调整后经营利
润率 15% （ GAAP 为 -3% ），算⼒承诺约 9009 亿美元，⻓期利润率⽬标 35% 以上。
摘要：
Anthropic 于 11 ⽉ 10 ⽇正式启动 IPO 路演，同步上线 42 分钟路演视频与 57 ⻚路演 PPT ，价格区间
定为每股 70 ⾄ 78 美元，计划于 11 ⽉ 18 ⽇定价、 11 ⽉ 19 ⽇在纳斯达克挂牌，股票代码 ANT 。
路演视频中，第⼀个出镜的是 CEO Dario Amodei 。他在开场约 30 秒就提到 AI 可能被⽤于制造⽣
物武器。他同时把 AI 称为 “ ⼈类历史上最有前景的技术 ” ，称其 “ 捕捉到了认知的本质 ” ，并强调安
全是公司最优先的事项。视频中，除 “Claude” 和 “ 模型 ” 外，出现最多的词是 “ 算⼒ ” ，共 49 次，

“ 安全 ” 相关词汇 27 次。
视频之外，公司同步放出 57 ⻚ PPT 。 PPT 显⽰，2026 年第三季度收⼊为 169.56 亿美元，是去年
同期的 12 倍以上，调整后经营利润率为 15% ，按 GAAP ⼝径为 -3% 。算⼒承诺约 9009 亿美元，
⻓期利润率⽬标 35% 以上。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

路演说得最多的词：算⼒
《商业内幕》请 ChatGPT 和 Claude 分别分析视频⽂字稿，统计⾼频词和主题。该报道称，剔
除 "Claude" （ 65 次）和 " 模型 " （ 58 次）后，" 算⼒ " 以 49 次居⾸，其后依次是客⼾（ 42 次）、⼯作
（ 39 次）、 AI （ 39 次）、前沿（ 32 次）。 " 安全 " 相关词汇出现 27 次，排第⼗。
按主题合并统计， " 模型与训练 " 以 83 次排第⼀， " 算⼒与基础设施 "81 次， " 财务表现与增⻓ "77
次， " 效率与价值 "69 次， " 客⼾与企业 "62 次， " 安全、对⻬、信任与⻛险 "55 次，排第六。
算⼒之所以占据这么⼤篇幅，与公司的资⾦需求直接相关。据 Anthropic 修订版 S-1 ，公司预计在
算⼒和基础设施上⽀出约 8,950 亿美元，其中近⼀半预计在 2030 年底前发⽣。
对于外界关⼼的安全问题，公司表⽰：
我们发现，安全并没有拖我们的后腿。恰恰因为我们能信任 Claude 的⼯作，才能把它的发现应

⽤到现实世界，让所有⼈受益。

### Page 3

收⼊：⼀年前单季收⼊约 14 亿美元，现在是 170 亿美元
PPT 显⽰，Anthropic 2025 年第三季度收⼊为 13.82 亿美元， 2026 年第三季度是 169.56 亿美
元。截⾄ 2026 年 10 ⽉，年化运⾏率收⼊约 810 亿美元。
按公司定义，运⾏率收⼊由两部分相加：最近 28 天的按量计费收⼊乘以 13 ，加上当⽉订阅费⽤
乘以 12 。
年度数据显⽰，公司 2023 年收⼊ 4,100 万美元， 2024 年 3.86 亿美元， 2025 年 45.85 亿美元。
2025 年净亏损 419.65 亿美元，附录称主要由可转换票据公允价值变动导致，原因是企业估值上
升。

### Page 4

公司对市场空间的判断是：2025 年全球知识⼯作所创造的经济价值超过 40 万亿美元，现在的收
⼊处于 " 早期阶段" 。这是公司⾃⼰的估算（以美国劳⼯统计局的职业⼯资数据为基础，再按国际
劳⼯组织的劳动收⼊占 GDP ⽐重推算到全球）。
模型：已发布 250 个模型、产品和功能
PPT ⽤⼀条曲线描述这段增⻓。曲线从 2023 年 3 ⽉商业化起步，沿途标出 Claude 2 、 Claude 3 、
Claude Code 等节点，终点是第三季度的约 170 亿美元。公司称，2026 年截⾄第三季度已发布
250 个模型、产品和功能。

### Page 5

客⼾：付费⼤客⼾⼀年内增⻓近 8 倍
PPT 显⽰，截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇，过去⼗⼆个⽉贡献⾄少 10 万美元收⼊的客⼾超过 11,900 家。 2025 年
底约 1,500 家， 2024 年底约 200 家， 2023 年底约 20 家。年⽀出超过 100 万美元的客⼾超过 1,900
家，超过 1,000 万美元的约 250 家。第三季度 79% 的收⼊来⾃前五⼤客⼾之外。
公司披露的净收⼊留存率约为 900% ，即同⼀批合格客⼾在本季度贡献的收⼊，约为⼀年前对应
季度的 9 倍。统计不含 Pro 、 Max 、 Ultra 等固定价格订阅⽤⼾。
PPT ⽤阶梯图描绘客⼾的扩张路径：先⽤于⼀个场景， Claude Code 扩展到相邻岗位，知识⼯作
者开始⽤ Cowork ，之后进⼊核⼼基础设施，最后嵌⼊客⼾⾃⼰的产品。
公司部分企业客⼾，包括 AIG 、百时美施贵宝、 DoorDash 、 Jane Street 、礼来、 Mercado
Libre 、 Mozilla 、 PayPal 、乐天、 SAP 、 Shopify 、 Travelers 、 UST 、 Verizon 和沃尔玛。同时使

⽤平台和⾄少⼀款产品的企业客⼾占⽐超过 50% ， 2025 年第四季度为 30% 。
销售⽅式上，公司称有三条路径：直销和应⽤ AI 团队、三⼤云⼚商及⽣态伙伴，以及⾯向中型企
业的 "Ode with Anthropic" 。
客⼾从⼀个使⽤场景起步，并迅速扩展，直⾄让 Claude 变得不可或缺。

### Page 6

超过 30% 的全球 2000 强企业、超过 50% 的财富 500 强企业，过去⼗⼆个⽉在 Claude 上的⽀出不
低于 10 万美元。
但这⼀⻚ PPT 的标题是 " 对最⼤型企业的渗透仍处早期 " ：财富 500 强客⼾在 Claude 上的平均⽀
出，占其总费⽤不到 0.1% ，⽀出最多的 10 家也不到 1% 。该统计只包含上市的财富 500 强客⼾，
使⽤的是它们公开财报中最近⼀个季度的总费⽤。
模型能⼒变化
⽀撑客⼾扩张的，是模型能⼒的变化。
2024 年主要是查找事实、修复漏洞和图像识别； 2025 年扩展到代码重构、多步骤⼯作流和深度
研究；⽬前列出的任务包括⾃主编程、智能体⼯作流、安全漏洞评估和监管申报⽂件起草。药
物发现、疾病诊断等被列为下⼀阶段⽅向。

### Page 7

产品形态同样发⽣了转变： Claude 从响应单次提⽰的⼯具，变成可在员⼯⾝边完成⻓周期复杂
⼯作的 " 虚拟协作者 " 。
收⼊涨 45 倍，算⼒费⽤涨约 10 倍
既然 " 算⼒ " 是视频⾥的最⾼频词， PPT ⾥的算⼒账就是最值得细看的部分。
从 2025 年第⼀季度到 2026 年第三季度，收⼊增⻓超过 45 倍，算⼒及基础设施费⽤增⻓约 10 倍。
公司把收⼊除以算⼒及基础设施费⽤定义为 " 算⼒效率 " ，这⼀⽐值从 0.40 倍升⾄ 1.72 倍，提⾼
4.3 倍。

### Page 8

但这个⽐值并⾮⼀路上升。第⼆季度为 2.08 倍，第三季度回落到 1.72 倍。第三季度算⼒及基础设
施费⽤为 98.63 亿美元。
公司解释，当季交付了额外算⼒，其中包括 50 亿美元的短期承诺算⼒，成本 " 明显⾼于 " 多年期承
诺下采购的可⽐算⼒。
这直接体现在利润率上。调整后经营利润率从 2025 年第⼀季度的 -221% 升到 2026 年第⼆季度的
19% ，第三季度回落⾄ 15% 。公司在脚注中把回落归因于上述 50 亿美元短期算⼒。
公司把算⼒视为跨多个合作伙伴的统⼀资源池，涉及 AWS Trainium 、 Google TPU 、 NVIDIA
GPU 和 AMD GPU 四类芯⽚，并把效率提升归因于算⼒倍增器研究和多芯⽚部署。
公司还披露， 7 位联合创始⼈均仍在公司。公司同时称⾃⼰是公益公司，⻓期利益信托⽬前由四
名⽆财务利益的受托⼈组成，⼀般有权选举董事会多数成员。这与视频中反复强调的 " 使
命 " 和 " 安全 " 相呼应。

### Page 9

在客⼾⼀侧，公司称模型效率提⾼后可以降低客⼾成本。同等⼯作负载下， Fable 5.1 相⽐ Fable
5 的客⼾成本低约 25% ， Opus 5.5 相⽐ Opus 5 低约 40% 。公司的表述是：
调整后利润率 15% ， GAAP 为 -3%
视频和 PPT ⾥反复出现的 " 利润率 " ，需要把两个⼝径分开看。
第三季度调整后经营收⼊ 24.63 亿美元，对应利润率 15% 。按 GAAP ⼝径，经营亏损 5.67 亿美
元，利润率 -3% 。
差额来⾃调整项：当季股权激励 10.7 亿美元，⾮现⾦慈善捐赠 19.6 亿美元。按公司定义，调整后
指标还不含此前的 Bartz 和解费⽤。 2025 年全年，调整后经营亏损 48.54 亿美元，调整后利润
率 -106% 。
更⾼的模型效率，让我们在保持利润率的同时降低客⼾成本。

### Page 10

第三季度净亏损 232.91 亿美元，其中可转换票据等公允价值变动 219.36 亿美元。
⻓期⽬标：调整后经营利润率 35% 以上
在此基础上，公司给出⻓期⽬标：调整后经营利润率 35% 以上。费⽤结构如下：
⾃⼰是第⼀个客⼾：⼯程师⼈均代码量是 2024 年的 45 倍以上
公司把⼈员费⽤下降归因于内部使⽤ Claude 。
2026 年第三季度，每位活跃⼯程师合并的代码⾏数，是 2024 年平均⽔平的 45 倍以上。这个倍数
在 2025 年第⼀季度为 1.3 倍， 2026 年第⼀季度为 6.2 倍，第⼆季度为 10.3 倍。公司同时称， 2026
年 9 ⽉代码库中超过 90% 的贡献由 Claude 编写。
占收⼊⽐例 2026 年第三季度⻓期稳态算⼒及基础设施 58% 约 45%–50% 销售及营销 14% 约 7%–
10% 调整后⼈员及其他运营费⽤ 13% 约 7%–10% 调整后经营利润率 15%35% 以上

### Page 11

公司把这条逻辑称为 " 闭环 " ：
此外，调整后⾮算⼒费⽤占收⼊的⽐例，从 2025 年第⼀季度的 70% 降⾄ 2026 年第三季度的
27% 。其中⼈员费⽤占⽐从 30% 降到 4% ，其他运营及⾏政费⽤从 33% 降到 10% ，销售、营销及
合作伙伴费⽤从 6% 升到 14% 。该⻚还披露，按标价计，内部使⽤ Claude 的规模从 2026 年 1 ⽉到
9 ⽉增⻓超过 250 倍。
过去⼗⼆个⽉⼈均收⼊超过 660 万美元，⼈数含全职和兼职员⼯。
每⼀代 Claude 都在帮助构建下⼀代，持续加快我们的开发节奏。

### Page 12

算⼒承诺约 9009 亿美元，上市前流动性 2160 亿美元
截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇，公司合同义务共 3,200 亿美元，其中采购承诺（主要是云服务商托管） 3,138 亿美
元。已签署但尚未开始的租赁为 4,512 亿美元，可取消或期限在 12 个⽉以内的最低消费为 174 亿
美元， 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇之后新签的协议为 1,057 亿美元。合计约 9,009 亿美元。
其中约 4,516 亿美元预计在 2030 年底前发⽣，⼏乎所有余额发⽣在 2031 ⾄ 2045 年，约 707 亿美元
在未来⼗⼆个⽉内到期。这些是未折现现⾦⽀付，不能与预测的算⼒费⽤直接对⽐，且不含未
约定固定⾦额的其他安排，公司估计这部分为 1,500 亿⾄ 1,750 亿美元。
资⾦⽅⾯，公司累计融资约 1,410 亿美元，现⾦及短期投资约 1,320 亿美元，上市前总流动性
2,160 亿美元，包括 820 亿美元战略融资额度和 148 亿美元未动⽤循环信贷额度。战略融资额度已
扣除同步私募中 Google 的 100 亿美元和 AMD 的 25 亿美元。

### Page 13

PPT 还拆分了资⾦构成：约 960 亿美元现⾦及现⾦等价物，约 360 亿美元短期投资，约 150 亿美
元信贷额度可⽤额度。
七项增⻓策略
投资⼈才与⽂化、投资算⼒、提升 Claude 的智能与安全、让 Claude" ⽆处不在 " 、扩⼤现有及新
客⼾的采⽤、把前沿能⼒拓展到新市场，以及开展战略交易与合作。

### Page 14

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂

Document 298: Anthropic逆市推进IPO！最快周二路演，冲刺下周四、五上市，估值或再打“八折”

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic逆市推进IPO！最快周二路演，冲刺下周四、五上市，估值或再打“八折”.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Anthropic 仍决定顶住市场压⼒推进 IPO ，最快于本周⼆开启路演，⽬标是让其股票在下周
四或周五开始交易。但在经历了 AI 公司股票的暴跌后，⼀些主要投资者现在希望⽬标估值
更低，部分潜在投资者讨论的估值已降⾄约 1.2 万亿⾄ 1.3 万亿美元，甚⾄有⼈考虑低⾄ 1.1
万亿美元。
摘要：
尽管 AI 板块近期遭遇剧烈抛售， Anthropic 仍决定顶住市场压⼒推进 IPO ，此次⼤规模上市将直
接测试公开市场对前沿 AI 资产的⻛险偏好与定价意愿。
据《华尔街⽇报》最新报道，⼀周前，投资者预期 Anthropic 的估值⽬标约为 1.5 万亿美元，但
在经历了 AI 公司股票的暴跌后，⼀些主要投资者现在希望⽬标估值更低。⽬前，该公司⾼管⼀
直在与⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利的银⾏家会⾯，商讨估值区间的最终确定。
据彭博 6 ⽇援引⼀位知情⼈⼠透露，部分潜在投资者讨论的估值已降⾄约 1.2 万亿⾄ 1.3 万亿美
元，甚⾄有⼈考虑低⾄ 1.1 万亿美元。
报道称， Anthropic 本周已安排与关键投资者会⾯，⽬标是让其股票在下周四或周五开始交易。

该公司仍有可能推迟估值⽬标并等待更有利的市场条件，但这可能会引发市场恐慌。
此次 IPO 的进展与最终定价不仅关乎 Anthropic ⾃⾝的融资规模，更将成为整个 AI 产业链估值的
⻛向标，直接影响市场对 AI 基础设施⾼昂资本开⽀及其商业化前景的重新评估。
冲刺感恩节前上市，估值预期⾯临下调
Anthropic 此次 IPO 的时间表已进⼊关键阶段。
据彭博社报道，根据相关规定，在 10 ⽉ 26 ⽇公开提交招股书后， Anthropic 最快可于 11 ⽉ 10 ⽇开
始正式 IPO 营销。若进展顺利，公司有望在 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇当周完成定价，赶在 11 ⽉ 26 ⽇美国感恩节
假期前登陆纳斯达克，股票代码为 “ANT” 。
然⽽，市场的剧烈波动使得估值成为各⽅博弈的核⼼。数⽉前，市场曾讨论过约 2 万亿美元的估
值数字，但 Anthropic 现在⼏乎肯定需要接受⼀个低得多的估值。据彭博 6 ⽇援引⼀位知情⼈⼠
透露，部分潜在投资者讨论的估值已降⾄约 1.2 万亿⾄ 1.3 万亿美元，甚⾄有⼈考虑低⾄ 1.1 万亿

美元。
对于投⾏⽽⾔，定价策略⾄关重要。如果⽬标设定过⾼，投资者需求可能受损并导致股价下
跌；若区间过低，公司和银⾏家将⾯临 “ 把钱留在桌⾯上 ” 的批评。在实际操作中，银⾏家有时会
⿎励公司提出略低于预期的价格区间，以便随后以投资者需求强劲为由上调价格。预计本周提
交的⽂件将详细说明计划出售的股票数量及价格，并可能披露已锁定按 IPO 价格认购的知名投资
者。
政治与市场双重阻⼒， AI 概念⾯临重估
Anthropic 此次 IPO ⾯临的挑战不仅是投资者对其⾃⾝估值的分歧，更受到整个 AI 产业链⻛险偏
好变化的影响。美国中期选举后，⼀批对 AI 持怀疑态度的⼈⼠掌权，并出台了可能减缓美国数据
中⼼发展的暂停令，导致纳斯达克综合指数成分股在⼀⽇内蒸发了 2.5 万亿美元市值。

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
521 收藏 分享到 :
市场波动曾推动芝加哥期权交易所的波动率指数（ VIX ）升破 25 ⾄ 28 上⽅。 Renaissance
Capital ⾼级策略师 Matt Kennedy 表⽰，当 VIX 处于 25 以上⽔平时，企业通常会考虑推迟 IPO 。此
外，投资者对巨额 AI 投资及其潜在回报缺乏清晰度的担忧⽇益增加，此前 Anthropic 的竞争对⼿
OpenAI 在最新⼀轮融资中筹集的资⾦不到最初⽬标的⼀半，估值也低于⽬标的 1.25 万亿美元。
不过，市场随后出现了⼀定程度的修复。标普 500 指数和费城半导体指数的反弹，以及 VIX 指数
的回落，可能为 Anthropic 继续推进上市提供了额外的信⼼。 Matt Kennedy 认为， Anthropic 的
IPO 不同于⼀般企业上市，当前市场环境未必⾜以迫使其推迟上市，但投资者对折价和估值的要

求可能更⾼。
业绩⾼增⻓遭遇放缓，投资者紧盯算⼒成本
估值争议的另⼀核⼼，是 Anthropic 能否证明⾃⾝的营收增⻓⾜以⽀撑庞⼤的上市估值。招股书
显⽰， Anthropic 第三季度营收达到 169.56 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 1127% 。但增⻓速度已出现放缓迹
象，第三季度环⽐增幅降⾄ 47% ，远低于第⼆季度 144% 的环⽐增速。
利润表现同样承压。第三季度， Anthropic 经营亏损 5.67 亿美元，净亏损达到 232.91 亿美元。业
绩转亏主要受到约 50 亿美元短期算⼒采购成本，以及约 20 亿美元员⼯捐赠匹配计划相关⾮现⾦
费⽤的影响。更令市场关注的是，公司披露的计算能⼒采购及基础设施承诺⽀出总额⾼达 8954
亿美元。
据路透社报道，投资者⽬前仍在等待 Anthropic 的 10 ⽉份营收数据，希望借此判断公司能否扭转
近期增⻓放缓的趋势。营收表现、企业客⼾使⽤量以及算⼒成本控制能⼒，将是路演期间投资
者重点追问的核⼼指标。
融资规模或创纪录，⾏业⻛向标意义凸显

作为⾸批以开发前沿 AI 模型为核⼼业务并尝试登陆公开市场的公司之⼀， Anthropic 此次发⾏的
规模和定价将为整个 AI ⾏业提供重要参照。
市场正密切关注 Anthropic 的 IPO 能否与 Elon Musk 旗下 SpaceX 创纪录的上市表现相媲美。
SpaceX 在此前的发⾏中估值约为 1.77 万亿美元，筹集资⾦规模在 750 亿美元⾄ 862 亿美元之间。
⽽据路透社报道， Anthropic 此次发⾏规模甚⾄可能达到 1000 亿美元。
由⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利牵头，另有 20 家银⾏参与的这笔超⼤型 IPO ，其实际认购情况将给出明确的
信号：在经历 AI 概念股⼤幅调整后，公开市场究竟愿意为前沿 AI 公司的⻓期增⻓潜⼒⽀付多⾼的
溢价。

2 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Stronger Growth, Costlier Money
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The latest GDP report and annual revisions show a sturdier U.S. economy than previously
reported. According to the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA), real GDP grew at a 2.2%
annualized pace in Q2 compared to 1.5% previously estimated, and Q1 was revised
from 2.1% to 2.5%. Both 2024 and 2025 were also revised slightly higher. Most of the

first half of the year revision was in categories related to the Artificial Intelligence (AI)
buildout, but real consumer spending has also been revised slightly higher, accelerating
from 0.7% annualized growth in Q1 to 3.8% in Q2 despite surging energy prices and
slow hiring.
Spending in July and August remained much stronger than labor market conditions alone
would suggest, also averaging 3.7% annualized growth. Upside revisions to net interest

income and personal saving suggest households have benefited from more income than
was previously apparent, with personal saving revised higher back to 2023. The new data,
according to the BEA, show the January to July 2026 savings rate averaged 4.8% versus
3.3% prerevision, strengthening consumer spending underpinnings despite moderate
hiring, high energy prices and rising borrowing costs.
The BEA update also included inflation methodological changes related to categories

inflated by the AI boom—such as portfolio management fees and computer software and
accessories. These improved the inflation picture to some extent—August “core” PCE
inflation was reduced by 0.3 ppts to 3% YoY, bringing it closer to “core” consumer price
index inflation, which was 2.5% YoY and 2% on a three-month annualized basis. While still
above the 2% Federal Reserve (Fed) target, “core” PCE now appears stable at 3% year over
year since January rather than accelerating. These methodological changes are expected

to dampen pass-through of AI-related price pressures into “core” inflation and effects of
stock-market swings on inflation.
They also produced an encouraging improvement in recent PCE inflation momentum.
Three-month annualized “core” PCE inflation slowed from 4.3% at the peak of tariff
effects in February to 2.1% in August. For durable goods, this rate was down from 5.8%
in February to 1.5% in August, and, for services ex-energy and healthcare, it slowed from

4.2% to 2.2%, for example. Inflation is volatile on a three-month change basis, so it
remains to be seen whether this moderation continues without further Fed tightening.
For now, the trends in the San Francisco Fed’s cyclical versus acyclical inflation measures
diverged again in August. As expected, given softening wage growth, prices most sensitive
to the labor market have eased further. At 3% YoY, this inflation category is down from
8% in early 2023 and 4.6% two years ago is back to prepandemic levels and is likely

to ease more (Exhibit 1A). In contrast, supply bottlenecks, high energy prices and other
industry-specific factors have kept acyclical inflation on an uptrend. As shown in Exhibit
1B, the relentless increase in freight costs could prolong upside pressure on this category.
The diesel market has tightened in recent months, boosting its price by 70% YoY,
consistent with Brent oil at around $200 per barrel and large reverberations across
a wide swath of goods and services prices. A government-policy-related decline in

available truck drivers is also raising transportation costs more than expected. So are
tight ocean shipping capacity and longer/more expensive voyages due to the U.S./Iran
War. Supply-chain stress also increased as companies rushed to front-run potential new
tariff increases.
On the positive side for the inflation outlook, unit labor costs (ULC) are up modestly
as robust productivity growth has absorbed much of the wage growth, limiting a major
source of domestic inflation. Upside productivity growth revisions are likely to further

reduce ULC, which could help shift focus from energy and AI-related inflation to
eventual disinflationary effects once oil and refined fuel prices start to ease. Sustained
productivity gains would support growth and margins while limiting inflation and the
need for much higher interest rates.
Investment Implications
We favor selective Equity exposure
to companies with durable demand
and pricing power, while remaining
cautious about businesses most

exposed to fuel, freight, and
financing costs. We would also
gradually increase long-duration
exposure.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Surging nominal growth has boosted corporate revenues (up 13% YoY in Q2, according to the
BEA) while contained unit labor costs helped raise margins and profits more than expected.
With labor costs declining as a share of revenues, investment-related tax incentives and
rock-bottom net interest expense, pre-tax nonfinancial corporate profits as measured in GDP
accounts rose 21% YoY in Q2. Total corporate profits after-tax were up 18.2% YoY, according to

the BEA.
Surging AI-related imports and investment are keeping the business and profits cycle on
an uptrend both here and abroad. According to BofA Global Research, the global economic
cycle backdrop improved further in September, as reflected in a broad-based increase in its
Global Wave1 indicator. Broadening U.S. manufacturing strength, fueled by ongoing AI-driven
investment, a healthier orders backlog and low inventories suggest that the expansion has

room to run—a constructive environment for earnings. Typically, a Global Wave upswing is
consistent with rising earnings revisions ratios and equity price uptrends. According to the
Global Wave, global earnings growth expectations remain exceptionally strong.
Still, absent a resolution of war-related energy and transportation disruptions, the same
industrial strength intensifies inflation pressures. A prolonged supply shock raises the risk of
restrictive interest rates and weakening economic growth. In this context, the surge in the

10-year Treasury yield above 5% is starting to reflect more than optimism about U.S. growth
and strengthening productivity outlook. Oil’s tightest recent correlation with 10-year Treasury
yields in 35 years suggests a common energy-price thread behind rising yields in developed
countries.
Bond market spillovers from normalizing Japan yields and capital repatriation are another
common thread. Higher yields on foreign sovereign bonds such as in Japan—which has

escaped stagnation and deflation by intensely pursuing investment-led growth—weaken the
relative appeal of Treasurys. That distinction matters for portfolios. A yield increase driven
mainly by durable productivity gains would come with a stronger outlook for future income
and earnings. A yield increase driven by cost shocks and greater uncertainty raises financing
costs without the same benefit.
In sum, the economy remains more resilient than expected, but the bond market is also

charging more for an uncertain inflation outlook given untethered government debt and
cooling global demand for Treasurys spurred by geopolitical tensions and Japan capital
repatriation. The case for much further tightening becomes stronger if wage growth
strengthens, productivity lags, and unit labor costs reaccelerate, causing cyclical inflation to
also pick up steam. The case becomes weaker if that measure stays contained while fuel and
freight pressures recede or rising Treasury yields reduce asset prices and economic growth.

4 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Yields, Yen and Iran: What Haunts the “House”
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
We recently highlighted the three-front challenge facing Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent:
engineering a stronger yen, containing the 10-year Treasury yield and securing a diplomatic
resolution to the conflict with Iran.

Bessent recently has delivered one of the year’s more memorable lines while discussing
Treasury policy and the yen: “I have asymmetric information. I am the house now.” 2 Yet several
challenges remain: the 10-year Treasury yield is near levels last seen in the early 2000s, the
yen remains structurally weaker, and a phased agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz
appears remote. The resulting strain on energy markets keeps inflation risks elevated.

Put differently, oil prices, Treasury yields and the dollar-yen exchange rate are all trending
against Bessent’s objectives. Below, we examine how each variable is haunting his “house.”
Haunting the “House” is the Yen. Since the U.S. and Japan mounted a record $98.7B
coordinated intervention in late July after the yen weakened to a 40-year low near 164 per
dollar, the currency has appreciated roughly 4% against the dollar. The move was further
supported on September 18 when the BoJ raised rates by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%,

their highest level since the mid-1990s (Exhibit 2A). However, the split 7–2 vote and Governor
Ueda’s limited forward guidance leave the path of further tightening uncertain. The key
question is whether additional BoJ hikes can produce sustained appreciation in the yen, or
merely reinforce the temporary relief provided by the summer intervention.
Bessent, meanwhile, has repeatedly signaled his preference for a stronger yen. Partly because
yen stability is also a U.S. financing concern. Persistent currency weakness could prompt

Japan to support its currency by selling foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys—which would
be an unwelcome prospect as Washington partially relies on foreign capital flows to fund its
large fiscal deficits. That potential “haunts” the Treasury Secretary.  At precisely the moment
Washington needs foreign capital to finance its enormous fiscal deficits, it would be inopportune
for one of America’s largest creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities.

Exhibit 2: Charting the “Yentervention” and U.S. Treasury Yields Surge Across the Curve.
A) The Yen Regains Some Strength. B) 5-Year Forward Yields Hold Above 5%.

Haunting the “House” are Yields. The surge in yields runs counter to a White House intent
on lowering borrowing costs. The benchmark 10-year yield has broken above the 5% threshold
and climbed to levels not seen since 2007. At around 5.30%, it stands more than a full
percentage point above where it began the year (Exhibit 2B). The level is striking but so is the
speed of the move: Stronger-than-expected economic data, including a survey showing U.S.

2 September 8, 2026.
Investment Implications
While some key variables are
complicating the macro backdrop
(i.e., higher-for-longer yields/oil)
the combination of strong earnings
growth, robust consumer demand
(real final sales to private domestic
purchasers grew at 4.6% for Q2) and
ongoing capital investment suggests
that the fundamental backdrop for
U.S. Equities remains constructive.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
business activity expanded at the fastest pace in more than five years, helped trigger an 18
bps surge in the 10-year yield—among its largest daily moves in decades. The 5-year yield also
topped 5% for the first time since 2007, while the 30-year yield reached 5.63%, its highest
level since 2002.
Compounding the move is inflation, running above the Fed’s 2% target for 67 months,

large fiscal deficits and the prospect of continued growth in Treasury issuance. Against this
backdrop, Treasury bought back $4.1 billion of long-dated debt, versus a maximum of $6
billion. That effort has done little to arrest the selloff. A subsequent auction priced nearly $4
billion of 7-year notes at 5.085%, the highest yield for that maturity in 33 years.
All in, the MOVE index, a key gauge of Treasury market volatility, jumped from 78 to 95 on

Wednesday September 23 and then to 104 the following day, its highest level since the early
days of the Iran war (Exhibit 3A).
Haunting the “House” is the Iran conflict. Early expectations that military pressure and
economic sanctions would quickly bring Iran to the negotiating table have yet to materialize.
Iranian officials have signaled that a full reopening of the Strait of Hormuz remains unlikely
while sanctions stay in place. Although talks between Washington and Tehran are continuing,

conflicting signals and lingering skepticism from both sides have kept tensions elevated.
As a result, oil prices have remained elevated in a range even as some Middle East supply
disruptions began to ease.
Startling is that Brent crude is up more than 60% this year, but refined product prices
have risen even faster. Ongoing disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz,
combined with Ukrainian strikes on Russian refining infrastructure have tightened global

supplies of crude oil and refined fuels. Saudi Arabia has restored some drone-disrupted flows,
providing roughly 3.5 million barrels a day of relief (roughly 3% of global consumption).
The squeeze is mostly acute in diesel markets, where inventories remain constrained and
prices continue to climb (Exhibit 3B). U.S. diesel prices recently surpassed $6.50 per gallon,
the highest level on record, just as Russia has extended restrictions on diesel exports into

October. Against this backdrop, proposals to ban U.S. fuel exports risks worsening the
imbalance. According to S&P Global, an export ban could reduce refinery runs by nearly 2
million barrels per day and significantly cut gasoline production, potentially driving gasoline
and jet fuel prices even higher. As long as tensions persist, the oil impulse remains a key
impediment to structurally lower inflation.
Investment implications. Treasury Secretary Bessent increasingly sits at the intersection of

monetary policy, fiscal policy, foreign policy and national security. We remain constructive on
the U.S. economy and overweight U.S. Equities but are watching as these fronts challenge the
narrative. There is a great deal riding on the “house” versus the key macro variables of today.

6 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
When It Comes to Attracting Foreign Capital, the U.S. Remains Exceptional
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
There may be no better vote of confidence in a country than the willingness of
foreigners to put their money there. And by that measurement, the U.S. remains the
exception—both portfolio and FDI inflows are currently running at record highs based

on the latest data. This dynamic is one key reason why we remain constructive on U.S.
assets over the longer term.
Notwithstanding all the handwringing about America’s fiscal deficits, tariffs and rising
government debt, global investors sunk some $942B in net purchases of U.S. Equities in
the 12 months to July, the highest rolling 12-month total on record. In Q2, net purchase
of U.S. Equities soared to $426B, up 62% from the same period a year ago (Exhibit 4A).

By country, flows were down from China—not surprisingly—but strong from Taiwan and
South Korea, who have taken to recycling their massive AI-generated trade surplus back
into the U.S. In the end, strong portfolio inflows suggest that international investors
remain willing to increase their exposure to the world's largest capital market—that’s
bullish for U.S. assets.
Meanwhile, Exhibit 4B highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be

inside the U.S., with FDI inflows totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year.
That is a staggering 103% jump from a year ago and means FDI inflows are on track for
a record year, estimated at $480B for 2026.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Two, within the $32 trillion-plus economy with extraordinarily consumer

purchasing power, foreign firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment
than just trade (exports). Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers,
semiconductors, power generation, etc., has created numerous potential investment
opportunities for many foreign firms that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging
capex cycle. Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly
developed technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.

In the end, double-barreled capital inflows speak to the absolute and relative
attractiveness of the U.S. economy and capital markets. Stay invested in America—the
rest of the world is.
Exhibit 4: U.S. Exceptionalism: Record Inflows of Portfolio and FDI Inflows.
A) Net Foreign Purchases of US Equities and Investment Funds. B) FDI Inflows to the U.S.
